```json
 "new_string": "export function kingSlimeAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {\n  // AI_015_KingSlime(NPC.cs:43436-43735)1:1 状态机:\n  //  ai0 = 落地充能计数(负值回充,≥0 起跳;5/6 态复用为缩放计时)\n  //  ai1 = 跳序 0→1→2→3→0(三小跳 + 一大跳)/5 缩身传送/6 淡入回归\n  //  ai2 = 周期传送计数(无视线||高差>160 时累计)\n  //  ai3 = 小史莱姆掉血水位(初值 lifeMax)\n  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = -100; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; }  // :43454-43464\n  if (e.ai3 === 0 && e.hp > 0) e.ai3 = e.maxHp;                          // :43450-43453\n  let num = 1;          // 体型倍率(5/6 态缩放 1↔0.5)\n  let flag2 = false;    // 传送态(5/6)=落地不充能\n  let flag3 = false;    // 完全缩没(60t 起,dontTakeDamage)\n  // 玩家死/远距 3000px(:43466-43490):TargetClosest 二次判定后仍失败 →\n  // EncourageDespawn(10) + 背对 + ai[2]=0/ai[0]=0/ai[1]=5 缩身传送世界角\n  const ksd = player ? Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy) : Infinity;\n  if (!player || player.dead || ksd > 3000) {\n    e.encourageDespawn(10);\n    if (player) e.facing = player.cx < e.cx ? 1 : -1;   // :43472-43479 转身背对\n    if (e.ai1 !== 5 && e.ai1 !== 6) {\n      e.ai2 = 0; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 5;\n      kingTpDest.set(e, { x: game.world.w * TILE, y: game.world.h * TILE });  // :43486-43487\n    }\n  }\n  // ---- 常规周期传送(:43491-43513):玩家活/timeLeft>10/计数≥300/非传送态/落地 →\n  //  计数清零进 5;antiCheese(localAI[0]≥360 或距>2000px)直落玩家脚底,否则 FindTeleportSpot\n  if (player && !player.dead && e.despawnTimer > 10 && e.ai2 >= 300 && e.ai1 < 5 && e.onGround) {\n    e.ai2 = 0; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 5;\n    let ac = kingAntiCheese.get(e) ?? 0;\n    const vec = Math.hypot(player.cx - e.cx, player.cy - e.cy);\n    let antiCheese = false;\n    if (ac >= 360 || vec > 2000) {\n      if (ac >= 360) { ac = 360; kingAntiCheese.set(e, ac); }   // :43505-43508 钳 360\n      antiCheese = true;\n    }\n    kingTpDest.set(e, antiCheese\n      ? { x: player.cx, y: player.y + player.h }   // :43404-43409 FindTeleportSpot(antiCheese)\n      : kingFindTeleportSpot(e, game, player));\n  }\n  // ---- 反挂机计数(:43514-43529,每 tick 无地面门):无视线||高差>160 →\n  //  ai[2]+1 且 localAI[0]+1;否则 localAI[0]−1 钳 0(曾写成恒 +1=语义反,2026-08-19)。\n  //  视线 = Center↔Center 连线(CanHitLine 0×0);高差 = Top.Y↔player.Bottom.Y ----\n  if (player) {\n    const los = canHit(game.world.store, e.cx, e.cy, 0, 0, player.cx, player.cy, 0, 0);\n    const gap = Math.abs(e.y - (player.y + player.h));\n    if (!los || gap > 160) {\n      e.ai2 += 1;\n      kingAntiCheese.set(e, (kingAntiCheese.get(e) ?? 0) + 1);\n    } else {\n      kingAntiCheese.set(e, Math.max(0, (kingAntiCheese.get(e) ?? 0) - 1));\n    }\n  }\n  if (e.ai1 === 5) {\n    // 缩身传送态(:43537-43573):num = clamp((60-ai0)/60)×0.5+0.5;60t 起 flag3\n    // (隐没无敌);==60 Gore734 王冠;≥60 Bottom 锚落位 → 6 号;未隐没时每帧 10 蓝尘\n    flag2 = true;\n    e.ai0 += 1;\n    num = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (60 - e.ai0) / 60)) * 0.5 + 0.5;\n    if (e.ai0 >= 60) flag3 = true;\n    if (e.ai0 === 60) {\n      game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy - e.h / 2, '#FFD060', 6, 2, { life: 40 }); // Gore 734 王冠(:43550)\n    }\n    if (e.ai0 >= 60) {\n      const dest = kingTpDest.get(e) ?? { x: game.world.w * TILE, y: game.world.h * TILE };\n      e.x = dest.x - e.w / 2;\n      e.y = dest.y - e.h;                          // base.Bottom = (localAI[1], localAI[2])(:43554)\n      e.ai1 = 6; e.ai0 = 0;\n    }\n    if (!flag3) game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#4E88FF', 10, 1.2, { life: 30 });  // :43564-43571\n  } else if (e.ai1 === 6) {\n    // 淡入回归态(:43574-43600):num = clamp(ai0/30)×0.5+0.5;30t 回战斗;到达尘 ×2 速\n    flag2 = true;\n    e.ai0 += 1;\n    num = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, e.ai0 / 30)) * 0.5 + 0.5;\n    if (e.ai0 >= 30) { e.ai1 = 0; e.ai0 = 0; }\n    game.spawnParticles(e.cx, e.cy, '#4E88FF', 10, 2.4, { life: 30 });  // :43594-43599\n  }\n  e.dontTakeDamage = flag3;      // :43601 dontTakeDamage = (hide = flag3)\n  e.squash = (1 - num) * 1.2;    // 体型倍率近似(缩放 0.5..1 ↔ squash 0.6..0)\n  if (e.onGround) {\n    // 落地(:43602-43663):vx 阻尼 0.8(<0.1 归零);非传送态充能起跳\n    e.vx *= 0.8;\n    if (e.vx > -0.1 && e.vx < 0.1) e.vx = 0;\n    if (!flag2) {\n      // :43611-43631 充能 +2/t + 血量分档(<80 +1/<60 +1/<40 +2/<20 +3/<10 +4 = +2..+13)\n      e.ai0 += 2;\n      if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.8) e.ai0 += 1;\n      if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.6) e.ai0 += 1;\n      if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.4) e.ai0 += 2;\n      if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.2) e.ai0 += 3;\n      if (e.hp < e.maxHp * 0.1) e.ai0 += 4;\n      if (e.ai0 >= 0) {\n        // 跳序(:43632-43656):ai1==3 → vy-13/vx+3.5dir/回充-200/回 0;\n        // ai1==2 → vy-6/vx+4.5dir/回充-120/ai1+1;其余 → vy-8/vx+4dir/回充-120/ai1+1\n        // (方向 = TargetClosest 的 direction,即玩家侧)\n        const dir = player ? (player.cx > e.cx ? 1 : -1) : (e.facing || 1);\n        e.facing = dir;\n        if (e.ai1 === 3) { e.vy = -13; e.vx += 3.5 * dir; e.ai0 = -200; e.ai1 = 0; }\n        else if (e.ai1 === 2) { e.vy = -6; e.vx += 4.5 * dir; e.ai0 = -120; e.ai1 += 1; }\n        else { e.vy = -8; e.vx += 4 * dir; e.ai0 = -120; e.ai1 += 1; }\n      }\n      // :43658-43661 ai0 ≥ -30 = 起跳前蹲伏动画段(aiAction=1,squash 视觉)\n    }\n  } else if (player) {\n    // 空中(:43664-43682):朝 direction 0.2/t 加速,限 |vx|<3(FTW 6);已反向时先 0.93 阻尼\n    const num7 = game.world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld ? 6 : 3;\n    const dir = e.facing || 1;\n    if ((dir === 1 && e.vx < num7) || (dir === -1 && e.vx > -num7)) {\n      if ((dir === -1 && e.vx < 0.1) || (dir === 1 && e.vx > -0.1)) e.vx += 0.2 * dir;\n      else e.vx *= 0.93;\n    }\n  }\n  // 重力+地形 = 共享段(UpdateNPC_UpdateGravity + MoveWhileDry):0.3 重力/高空衰减/落速钳\n  e.applyGravity(game);\n  moveAndCollide(e, game.world, e.vx, e.vy);\n  if (e.hp <= 0) return;\n  // ---- 掉血 5% 出小史莱姆(:43708-43734):水位 ai3 每越线补记;1-3 只;\n  //  经典 1 号蓝史莱姆,专家 1/4 概率换 535(:43720-43723);体内随机点出、\n  //  初速 Next(-15,16)*0.1 / Next(-30,1)*0.1、ai[0]=-1000*Next(3) ----\n  const num10 = e.maxHp * 0.05;\n  if (e.hp + num10 < e.ai3) {\n    e.ai3 = e.hp;\n    const expert = mainExpertMode(game.world);\n    const n = 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);   // Next(1,4) = 1..3\n    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {\n      const s = spawnPart(game, e, expert && Math.floor(Math.random() * 4) === 0 ? 535 : 1);\n      if (s) {\n        s.x = e.x + Math.random() * Math.max(1, e.w - 32);\n        s.y = e.y + Math.random() * Math.max(1, e.h - 32);\n        s.vx = (Math.floor(Math.random() * 31) - 15) * 0.1;\n        s.vy = (Math.floor(Math.random() * 31) - 30) * 0.1;\n        s.ai0 = -1000 * Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);\n        s.ai1 = 0;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}"
}
```

```
function collideGround(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks) {
  const st = game.world.store;
  const tx = Math.floor(e.cx / TILE);
  const ty = Math.floor((e.y + e.h) / TILE);
  if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && st.isSolid(tx, ty) && e.vy >= 0) {
    e.y = ty * TILE - e.h;
    e.vy = 0;
    e.onGround = true;
  } else {
    e.onGround = false;
  }
  const ty2 = Math.floor(e.y / TILE);
  if (st.inBounds(tx, ty2) && st.isSolid(tx, ty2) && e.vy < 0) {
    e.y = (ty2 + 1) * TILE;
    e.vy = 0;
  }
  const tx2 = Math.floor((e.x + e.w) / TILE);
  if (st.inBounds(tx2, Math.floor(e.cy / TILE)) && st.isSolid(tx2, Math.floor(e.cy / TILE)) && e.vx > 0) {
    e.x = tx2 * TILE - e.w; e.vx = 0;
  }
  const tx3 = Math.floor(e.x / TILE);
  if (st.inBounds(tx3, Math.floor(e.cy / TILE)) && st.isSolid(tx3, Math.floor(e.cy / TILE)) && e.vx < 0) {
    e.x = (tx3 + 1) * TILE; e.vx = 0;
  }
}
```

```json
 "new_string": "export function queenBeeAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {\n  // 首帧复位(ai0 默认 -1120 是史莱姆族约定,原版 ai[0] 从 0 起步)\n  if (!e.bInit) { e.bInit = true; e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; la(e)[0] = 0; }\n  // 玩家失效 = 原版 TargetClosest 落空后的 dead4(:30316-30319)\n  const dead4 = !player || player.dead;\n  // 专家分支一(:30313-30318):defense = defDefense + (int)(20×(1-血比))——\n  // 基值缓存 la[3](蜂后 localAI[3] 原版未用)\n  const expert = mainExpertMode(game.world);\n  if (la(e)[3] === 0) la(e)[3] = e.def.defense;\n  if (expert) e.def.defense = la(e)[3] + Math.trunc(20 * (1 - e.hp / e.maxHp));\n  // 暴怒度 num658(:30321-30333):地表 +1 / 非丛林 +1 / getGoodWorld +0.5(:30331-30334)。\n  // ZoneJungle = Game.scene 窗口计数(丛林砖 60/61/62/74/226/225 ≥140 且非地狱层)\n  let num658 = 0;\n  if (e.y / TILE < game.world.groundLevel) num658 += 1;\n  if (!game.scene?.zoneJungle) num658 += 1;\n  if (game.world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld) num658 += 0.5;"
}
```

````
For case 1: aim lead + fire 462 (35 dmg, 8 speed) at dur-14/dur-7/dur, sound NPC_Hit_6 at dur-35. Fire from center (eye ellipse offset not modeled for core fallback — spawn at center; comment).

For case 3: spiral 452:
```ts
    case 3: { // 幻影之眼 452 螺旋(:38508-38572,曾误接三连矢)

```
Wait — check vanilla: num1312 = Lerp(8, 20, (num1294-45)/140) — used for the EYE's velocity (movement), not projectile. vector227 = Normalize(vector225) × 8 — projectile speed 8 ✓. vector226 = center + normalize(vector225) × vector218.Length()×0.4 — vector218=(30,30), length = 42.43, ×0.4 = 16.97. So spawn at center + dir×16.97 along eye angle ✓. ai3 param = visual rotation — Arrow probably ignores; fine.

Also the `if ((num1294 - 15f - 30f) % 10f == 0f)` gate = (inSlot - 45) % 10 === 0. And inSlot 45..184 fires at 45,55,...,175 = 14 shots ✓.

The angle: localAI[0] starts from wherever (0 for fresh). Our mlBarrageState = { ang: 0, rate: 0 } per-core WeakMap.

Also case 1 needs the aim with lead:
```ts

```

Keep case 2 (454) and case 4 (455 deathray) as-is (case 4 already exact-ish).

Just remove the trailing `return;`. f2T/f3T default 0 → frames advance to 0 ✓.
````

```json
 "new_string": "export function sharkronAI(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, player: Player | null): void {\n  if (!e.bInit) {\n    e.bInit = true;\n    e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 0;\n    e.spawnAlpha = 255; e.alphaFade = false; // 渐显由本 AI 按 :35228/:35267 驱动\n  }\n  // :35200-35205 玩家失效 → TargetClosest(faceTarget:false)+direction=1 重瞄,\n  // AI 照常运转(原版对尸位坐标继续扑,不消散——曾 dead=true 即散 = 存续断裂,\n  // 2026-08-19 台账 E 修;本仓单玩家无处重瞄 → 置 direction=1、维持现速滑行)\n  if (!player || player.dead) {\n    e.facing = 1;\n    player = null;\n  }\n  const RISE_T = 90; // num1077\n  if (e.ai0 === 0) {\n    e.iframes = 2;              // dontTakeDamage(:35211)\n    e.vx = 0;\n    e.vy = e.ai3;               // velocity.Y = ai[3](:35212)\n    if (e.vanillaId === 373) {\n      e.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, e.spawnAlpha - 6); // :35228 渐显\n      // :35222-35227 贴图倾斜 rotation = vy·spriteDirection·0.1(钳 ±0.2)\n      e.spin = clamp(e.vy * -e.facing * 0.1, -0.2, 0.2);\n    }\n    e.ai1 += 1;\n    if (e.ai1 >= RISE_T) {\n      // :35242-35261 蓄力期满:16 速扑咬(撞块内则 ai1=0 延后起扑);\n      // :35250 PlaySound(4,…,19)=NPCHit style19 起扑音(2026-08-19 台账 E 补)\n      e.ai0 = 1;\n      e.ai1 = inSolid(game, e) ? 0 : 1;\n      game.playSfxFiles(['NPC_Hit_19'], 1, e.cx, e.cy);\n      if (player) {\n        const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;\n        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n        e.vx = (dx / d) * 16;\n        e.vy = (dy / d) * 16;\n        e.facing = dx < 0 ? -1 : 1;\n        e.spin = Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx) + (e.facing < 0 ? Math.PI : 0);\n      }\n    }\n  } else {\n    // ai0 === 1(:35264-35316)\n    if (e.ai1 < 1 && !inSolid(game, e)) e.ai1 = 1;\n    if (inSolid(game, e)) e.spawnAlpha = Math.min(150, e.spawnAlpha + 15); // :35280-35284\n    if (e.ai1 >= 1) {\n      e.spawnAlpha = Math.max(0, e.spawnAlpha - 60); // :35288 渐显\n      e.ai1 += 1;\n      if (inSolid(game, e)) {\n        // :35295-35303 撞墙即死:PlaySound(DeathSound)——372/373 SetDefaults\n        // (:13666-13688)DeathSound=NPCDeath1 → NPC_Killed_1\n        game.playSfxFiles(['NPC_Killed_1'], 1, e.cx, e.cy);\n        e.dead = true;\n        return;\n      }\n    }\n    if (e.ai1 >= 60) e.applyGravity(game); // :35307-35309 恢复重力(共享 UpdateNPC_UpdateGravity)\n    e.spin = Math.atan2(e.vy, e.vx) + (e.facing < 0 ? Math.PI : 0); // :35311-35315\n  }"
}
```

```
export function mainDifficulty(world: DifficultyWorldView | null | undefined): number {
  let num: number = GDL.CLASSIC;
  if (world) {
    // 旅程强度滑杆 override（Main.cs:17241 IsJourneyMode + :2771-2773 HasValue →
    // 直接取值覆盖 GameMode 档；GameMode==3 即 IsJourneyMode Main.cs:2696）
    if (world.difficulty === 3) {
      num = strengthOfDifficultySlider(world.journeyPowers?.difficultySlider ?? 0);
    } else if (world.difficulty === 1) num = GDL.EXPERT;
    else if (world.difficulty === 2) num = GDL.MASTER;
    if (world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld) num += 1;
  }
  return num;
}

/** Main.expertMode（Main.cs:2762）：Difficulty >= Expert（getGoodWorld 抬档后同样生效；
 *  旅程强度滑杆 ≥2× 同样置位——Difficulty 轴语义） */
export function mainExpertMode(world: DifficultyWorldView | null | undefined): boolean {
  return mainDifficulty(world) >= GDL.EXPERT;
}

/** Main.masterMode（Main.cs:2760）：Difficulty >= Master */
export function mainMasterMode(world: DifficultyWorldView | null | undefined): boolean {
  return mainDifficulty(world) >= GDL.MASTER;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// NPCID.Sets（NPCID.cs：Factory.CreateBoolSet(...) 等价"集合包含"）
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
/** NeedsExpertScaling（:4799）：命中即强制进 ScaleStats（无视 lifeMax/damage 门） */

```

     // 距玩家 640px → 全阶梯\n    head.ai1 = 1; head.ai2 = 5; head.vx = 0; head.vy = 0;\n    skeletronPrimeAI(head, h.hooks, h.player as never);\n    expect(Math.hypot(head.vx, head.vy)).toBeCloseTo(6 * 1.05 * Math.pow(1.1, 10), 3);\n  });\n});\n\n// ===========================================================================\n// 史莱姆王(NPC.cs:43436-43735)\n// ===========================================================================\ndescribe('史莱姆王跳跃循环/反挂机计数/小史莱姆(2026-08-19 台账 E)', () => {\n  const jump = (expert: boolean, ai1: number) => {\n    const h = makeHooks(makeWorld(expert), P);\n    const e = spawn(h, 50, 100, FLOOR - 6); y < FLOOR; y++) { w.store.setTile(105, y, STONE); w.store.setTile(106, y, STONE); }\n    const e = spawn(h, 50, 100, FLOOR - 6);\n    e.ai0 = -200; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = 0;\n    e.onGround = true;\n    for (let t = 0; t < 302; t++) {\n      e.onGround = true;\n      kingSlimeAI(e, h.hooks, h.player as never);\n      if (e.ai1 === 5) break;\n    }\n    expect(e.ai1).toBe(5);                    // ai2 满 300 → 缩身传送态\n    // 有视线:ai2 不累计\n    const h2 = makeHooks(makeWorld(false), P);\n    const e2 = spawn(h2, 50, 100, FLOOR - 6);\n    e2.ai0 = -200; e2.ai1 = 0; e2.ai2 = 0; e2.ai3 = 0;\n    for (let t = 0; t < 50; t++) {\n      e2.onGround = true;\n      kingSlimeAI(e2, h2.hooks, h2.player as never);\n    }\n    expect(e2.ai2).toBe(0);\n    expect(e2.ai1).not.toBe(5);\n  });\n\n  it('掉血 5% 出 1-3 只小史莱姆(经典 1 号,专家 1/4 出 535;:43708-43734)', () => {\n    for (const expert of [false, true]) {\n      const h = makeHooks(makeWorld(expert), P);\n      const e = spawn(h, 50, 100, FLOOR - 6);\n      e.ai0 = -200; e.ai1 = 0; e.ai2 = 0; e.ai3 = e.maxHp;\n      e.onGround = true;\n      e.hp = Math.floor(e.maxHp * 0.9);       // 掉 10% → 至少两轮越线,取首轮\n      kingSlimeAI(e, h.hooks, h.player as never);\n      const minions = h.enemies.filter((n) => n.vanillaId === 1 || n.vanillaId === 535);\n      expect(minions.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);\n      expect(minions.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(3);\n      for (const m of minions) {\n        if (!expert) expect(m.vanillaId).toBe(1);\n        else expect([1, 535]).toContain(m.vanillaId);\n        expect(m.ai0 % 1000).toBe(0);         // -1000*Next(3)\n        expect(m.ai1).toBe(0);\n      }\n      expect(e.ai3).toBe(e.hp);               // 水位补记\n    }\n  });\n});\n\n// ===========================================================================\n// 蜂后(NPC.cs:30312-30998)\n// ===========================================================================\ndescribe('蜂后专家分支(2026-08-19 台账 E)', () => {\n  it('专家防御 +20×(1-血比)(:30313-30318)', () => {\n    const h = makeHooks(makeWorld(true), P);\n    const bee = spawn(h, 222, 100, 100);\n    bee.ai0 = 4;       // 玩家上方 → 满足上位发射门\n    bee.ai0 = 3; bee.ai1 = 13; bee.ai2 = 0;\n    bee.hp = Math.floor(bee.maxHp * 0.05);\n    queenBeeAI(bee, h.hooks, h.player as never);\n    expect(bee.ai1).toBe(14);                 // 14 % 15 == 14 → 触发\n    const shots = h.projectiles.filter((p) => p.projId === 719);\n    expect(shots.length).toBe(1);\n    expect(Math.hypot(shots[0].vx, shots[0].vy)).toBeCloseTo(13, 1);   // 8+2+3\n  });\n});\n\n// ===========================================================================\n// 世纪之花弹伤 Remap(NPC.cs:32123)\n// ===========================================================================\ndescribe('世花弹伤专家 ×0.9 Remap(:32123)', () => {\n  const fireOnce = (expert: boolean) => {\n    const h = makeHooks(makeWorld(expert), P);\n    const e = spawn(h, 262, 100, 96);\n    e.hp = Math.floor(e.maxHp * 0.85);        // >80% → 恒种子 275(22 伤档)\n    let guard = 0;\n    while (h.projectiles.length === 0 && guard++ < 300) {\n      planteraAI(e, h.hooks, h.player as never);\n    }\n    return h.projectiles[0];\n  };\n  it('经典 22 / 专家 trunc(22×0.9)=19(两级相乘:Remap × 命中端 hostile 缩放)', () => {\n    expect(fireOnce(false).damage).toBe(22);\n    expect(fireOnce(true).damage).toBe(19);\n  });\n});\n\n// ===========================================================================\n// 月亮领主(NPC.cs:36774-38128)\n// ===========================================================================\ndescribe('月总核心 Lerp/清弹/兜底/环境吼 + 头 -2 帧推进(2026-08-19 台账 E)', () => {\n  it('死亡演出 Lerp amount=0.98(命名参数陷阱,:36928):vx 一步收敛到 2%', () => {\n    const h = makeHooks(makeWorld(false), P);\n    const core = spawn(h, 398, 100, 100);\n    core.ai0 = 2; core.ai1 = 0; core.ai2 = 0;\n    const head = spawn(h, 396, 100, 90);\n    head.ai0 = -2; head.ai1 = 0; head.ai2 = 0; head.ai3 = core.id;\n    head.spin = 5;\n    moonLordHeadAI(head, h.hooks, h.player as never);\n    expect(head.ai0).toBe(-2);\n    expect(head.def.damage).toBe(80);\n    expect(head.spin).not.toBe(5); t < 320; t++) {\n      e.onGround = true;\n      kingSlimeAI(e, h.hooks, h.player as never);\n      if (e.ai1 === 5) break;\n    }\n    expect(e.ai1).toBe(5);                    // ai2 满 300 → 缩身传送态\n    // 有视线:ai2 不累计\n    const h2 = makeHooks(makeWorld(false), P);\n    const e2 = spawn(h2, 50, 100, FLOOR - 6);\n    e2.ai0 = -200; // 注入（Game.afterWorldLoad；对应原版 Main 静态单例）。
```
// ============================================================================
import type { World } from '../world/World';

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// GameDifficultyLevel（GameDifficultyLevel.cs:8-16）：档位是【浮点值】不是枚举序号
//   Journey 0.5 / Classic 1 / Expert 2 / Master 3 / Legendary 4
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export const GDL = {
  JOURNEY: 0.5,
  CLASSIC: 1,
  EXPERT: 2,
  MASTER: 3,
  LEGENDARY: 4,
} as const;

/** float32（C# float 字面量/强转位）。JS number 是 float64，在原版显式 (float) 处对齐 */
export const f32 = (v: number): number => Math.fround(v);

/** C# Math.Round(double)（默认 MidpointRounding.ToEven 银行家舍入：.5 → 就近偶数；
 *  JS Math.round 是 .5 远离零舍入，逐半值处会差 1） */
export const roundCS = (v: number): number => {
  const f = Math.floor(v);
  const d = v - f;
  if (d < 0.5) return f;
  if (d > 0.5) return f + 1;
  return f % 2 === 0 ? f : f + 1;
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Utils.GetLerpValue / Remap（Utils.cs:283-320，Remap 重载默认 clamped=true）
export const lerpF = (a: number, b: number, t: number): number => f32(a + f32(b - a) * t);

/** Utils.Lerp(double)（:246：a + (b-a)*t，双精度）——ByPlayerCount 的 balance 混合用 */
export const lerp = (a: number, b: number, t: number): number => a + (b - a) * t;

/** Utils.Remap（:313，默认 clamped=true）——float 重载入参先 (float) 对齐 */
export function remap(fromValue: number, fromMin: number, fromMax: number, toMin: number, toMax: number): number {
  return lerpF(f32(toMin), f32(toMax), getLerpValue(f32(fromMin), f32(fromMax), f32(fromValue), true));
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// GameDifficultyData.LinearCurve（GameDifficultyData.cs:16-60）：
//   keys 升序；Sample 找到 value 落入的相邻键区间做线性插值，
//   ≤首键输入取首键输出、≥尾键输入取尾键输出
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export interface CurveKey { input: number; output: number }

```

```
export function scaleStats(
  s: ScalingStats,
  ctx: ScaleStatsWorld = scaleStatsWorld(),
  activePlayersCount?: number | null,
  strengthOverride?: number | null,
): void {
  // 入口门（:18083）：NeedsExpertScaling 白名单 或 通用门
  //   lifeMax>5 && damage!=0 && !friendly && !townNPC
  //   → **Boss 不豁免**（boss 不在排除条件里）；ProjectNPC（25/30/…）靠白名单进入
  if (NEEDS_EXPERT_SCALING.has(s.type) || (s.lifeMax > 5 && s.damage !== 0 && !s.friendly && !s.townNPC)) {
    s.difficulty = strengthOverride != null ? strengthOverride : ctx.difficulty;   // :18086
    if (s.difficulty >= GDL.EXPERT && ctx.hardMode) scaleStatsForExpertHardmode(s, ctx);  // :18087-18089
    scaleStatsByDifficulty(s, ctx);                                                 // :18090
    if (s.difficulty >= GDL.EXPERT) {
      scaleStatsByPlayerCount(s, activePlayersCount != null ? activePlayersCount : ctx.activePlayerCount);  // :18091-18094
    }
    // :18096-18100 弹体 NPC 以外 lifeMax 下限 6（经典档同样生效）
    if ((s.type < 0 || !PROJECTILE_NPC.has(s.type)) && s.lifeMax < 6) s.lifeMax = 6;
    // :18101-18103 defDamage/defDefense/defLifeMax 快照 → 调用侧另存 baseDamage/baseDefense
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// NPC.ScaleStats_ByDifficulty（NPC.cs:18106-18116）
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function scaleStatsByDifficulty(s: ScalingStats, ctx: ScaleStatsWorld): void {
  if (s.type < 0 || !PROJECTILE_NPC.has(s.type)) {                       // :18108-18112
    s.lifeMax = Math.trunc(f32(s.lifeMax * sampleCurve(ENEMY_MAX_LIFE_MULTIPLIER, s.difficulty)));
    s.value = Math.trunc(f32(s.value * sampleCurve(ENEMY_MONEY_DROP_MULTIPLIER, s.difficulty)));
  }
  s.damage = getAttackDamageScaledByDifficulty(s.damage, s.difficulty);  // :18113
  s.knockBackResist = getKnockbackMultiplierScaledByDifficulty(s.knockBackResist, s.difficulty);  // :18114
  scaleStatsByDifficultyTweaks(s, ctx);                                  // :18115
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// NPC.ScaleStats_ByDifficulty_Tweaks（NPC.cs:18118-18447）
//   num=伤害系数（Classic→Expert）/ num2=生命 Classic→Expert / num3=生命 Expert→Master
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function scaleStatsByDifficultyTweaks(s: ScalingStats, ctx: ScaleStatsWorld): void {

  let num2 = 1.0;  // lifeMax (classic→expert)
  let num3 = 1.0;  // lifeMax (expert→master)
```
